Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The topology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 278 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 863 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to topology theory, we decompose the problem into 278 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 863. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
